===== M0001 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(Pair<JSError, CheckLevel>,Pair<JSError, CheckLevel>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 123-174
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |     public int compare(Pair<JSError, CheckLevel> p1,
124 |         Pair<JSError, CheckLevel> p2) {
125 |       // null is the smallest value
126 |       if (p2 == null) {
127 |         if (p1 == null) {
128 |           return 0;
129 |         } else {
130 |           return P1_GT_P2;
131 |         }
132 |       }
133 | 
134 |       // check level
135 |       if (p1.second != p2.second) {
136 |         return p2.second.compareTo(p1.second);
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // sourceName comparison
140 |       String source1 = p1.first.sourceName;
141 |       String source2 = p2.first.sourceName;
142 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
143 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
144 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
145 |           return sourceCompare;
146 |         }
147 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
148 |         return P1_LT_P2;
149 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
150 |         return P1_GT_P2;
151 |       }
152 |       // lineno comparison
153 |       int lineno1 = p1.first.lineNumber;
154 |       int lineno2 = p2.first.lineNumber;
155 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
156 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
157 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
158 |         return P1_LT_P2;
159 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
160 |         return P1_GT_P2;
161 |       }
162 |       // charno comparison
163 |       int charno1 = p1.first.getCharno();
164 |       int charno2 = p2.first.getCharno();
165 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
166 |         return charno1 - charno2;
167 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
168 |         return P1_LT_P2;
169 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
170 |         return P1_GT_P2;
171 |       }
172 |       // description
173 |       return p1.first.description.compareTo(p2.first.description);
174 |     }

===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
59 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
60 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
61 |     return s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q");
62 |   }

/**

/**

   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the

786 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
787 |                           parser.addWarning(
788 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
789 |                         }
790 |                         break;
791 | 
792 |                       case TYPEDEF:
793 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
794 |                           parser.addWarning(
795 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
796 |                         }
797 |                         break;
798 |                     }
799 | 
800 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
801 |                   }
802 |                   continue retry;
803 |               }
804 |             }
805 |           }
806 |           break;
807 | 
808 |         case EOC:
809 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
810 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
811 |           }
812 | 
813 |           return true;
814 | 
815 |         case EOF:
816 |           // discard any accumulated information
817 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
818 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
819 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
820 |           return false;
821 | 
822 |         case EOL:
823 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
824 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
825 |           }
826 |           token = next();
827 |           continue retry;
828 | 
829 |         default:
830 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
831 |             token = next();
832 |             continue retry;
833 |           } else {
834 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
835 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
836 |             continue retry;
837 |           }
838 |       }
839 | 
840 |       // next token
841 |       token = next();
842 |     }
843 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.parseAllJsDocInfo(Node,Comment[],int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 190-231
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses all temporary JsDoc strings in this node and all its children
   * recursively as well. Assumes the remaining JsDoc strings are contained in
   * pre-order with skips allowed, in the given comments, after the given index.
   *
   * @param node The current node to start parsing at.
   * @param comments An array of all comments in the source.
   * @param ci Current index into the array of comments.
   *
   * @return Current index into the array of comments after parsing this node.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private int parseAllJsDocInfo(Node node, Comment[] comments, int ci) {
191 |     if (ci >= comments.length) {
192 |       // There are no comments left.
193 |       return ci;
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     // Parse the JsDoc string on the current node, if any.
197 |     if (node.getProp(TMP_JSDOC_PROP) != null) {
198 |       String jsDoc = (String) node.getProp(TMP_JSDOC_PROP);
199 | 
200 |       // Find the match of the JsDoc string in the array of comments.
201 |       while (comments[ci].getCommentType() != JSDOC ||
202 |           !comments[ci].getValue().equals(jsDoc)) {
203 |         ci++;
204 |         Preconditions.checkState(ci < comments.length);
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       JSDocInfo info = parseJSDocInfo(jsDoc, comments[ci].getLineno(),
208 |           comments[ci].getAbsolutePosition());
209 |       node.setJSDocInfo(info);
210 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
211 |         if (node.getType() == Token.NAME) {
212 |           registry.identifyEnumName(node.getString());
213 |         } else if (node.getType() == Token.VAR &&
214 |             node.getChildCount() == 1) {
215 |           registry.identifyEnumName(node.getFirstChild().getString());
216 |         } else if (node.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
217 |           registry.identifyEnumName(node.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       ci++;
222 |       node.removeProp(TMP_JSDOC_PROP);
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     // Recurse on the children.
226 |     for (Node child : node.children()) {
227 |       ci = parseAllJsDocInfo(child, comments, ci);
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     return ci;
231 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1239-1252
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1239 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1240 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1241 |       int index = 0;
1242 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1243 |            current != null;
1244 |            current = current.getNext()) {
1245 |         if (current == node) {
1246 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1247 |         }
1248 |         index++;
1249 |       }
1250 |     }
1251 |     return false;
1252 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 796-823
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
797 |     // unknown
798 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
799 |       return true;
800 |     }
801 |     // equality
802 |     if (thisType.equals(thatType)) {
803 |       return true;
804 |     }
805 |     // all type
806 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
807 |       return true;
808 |     }
809 |     // unions
810 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
811 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
812 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
813 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
814 |           return true;
815 |         }
816 |       }
817 |     }
818 |     // named types
819 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
820 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
821 |     }
822 |     return false;
823 |   }

427 |     @Override
428 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
429 |       if (lineBreak) {
430 |         if (sawFunction) {
431 |           startNewLine();
432 |           sawFunction = false;
433 |         }
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
437 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
438 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
439 |       int len = code.length();
440 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
441 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
442 |         if (ch == ';') {
443 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
444 |         }
445 |       }
446 |       maybeCutLine();
447 |     }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.

Method code:
453 |     @Override
454 |     void maybeCutLine() {
455 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
456 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
457 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
458 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
459 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;

467 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
335 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
336 |     if (prototype == null) {
337 |       return false;
338 |     }
339 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
340 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
341 |       return false;
342 |     }
343 | 
344 |     this.prototype = prototype;
345 | 
346 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
347 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
348 |       if (superClass != null) {
349 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
350 |       }
351 |     }
352 |     return true;
353 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 477-500
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
477 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |       JSDocInfo info;
479 |       int valueNodeType;
480 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
481 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
482 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
483 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
484 |           break;
485 |         case Token.VAR:
486 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
487 |           if (info == null) {
488 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
489 |           }
490 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
491 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
492 |           break;
493 |         default:
494 |           return false;
495 |       }
496 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
497 |       return info != null &&
498 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
499 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
500 |     }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.flattenReferencesTo(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 320-346
Documentation:
/**
   * Flattens all references to a collapsible property of a global name except
   * its initial definition.
   *
   * @param n A global property name (e.g. "a.b" or "a.b.c.d")
   * @param alias The flattened name (e.g. "a$b" or "a$b$c$d")
   */
Method code:
320 |   private void flattenReferencesTo(Name n, String alias) {
321 |     if (n.refs != null) {
322 |       String originalName = n.fullName();
323 |       for (Ref r : n.refs) {
324 |         Node rParent = r.node.getParent();
325 | 
326 |         // There are two cases when we shouldn't flatten a reference:
327 |         // 1) Object literal keys, because duplicate keys show up as refs.
328 |         // 2) References inside a complex assign. (a = x.y = 0). These are
329 |         //    called TWIN references, because they show up twice in the
330 |         //    reference list. Only collapse the set, not the alias.
331 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(r.node, rParent) &&
332 |             (r.getTwin() == null || r.isSet())) {
333 |           flattenNameRef(alias, r.node, rParent, originalName);
334 |         }
335 |       }
336 |     }
337 | 
338 |     // Flatten all occurrences of a name as a prefix of its subnames. For
339 |     // example, if {@code n} corresponds to the name "a.b", then "a.b" will be
340 |     // replaced with "a$b" in all occurrences of "a.b.c", "a.b.c.d", etc.
341 |     if (n.props != null) {
342 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
343 |         flattenPrefixes(alias, p, 1);
344 |       }
345 |     }
346 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1625-1641
Documentation:
/**
     * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
     * each property separated by dots.  If the node ultimately under the left
     * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
     *
     * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
     * of the name and properties.
     */
Method code:
1625 |     public String getQualifiedName() {
1626 |         if (type == Token.NAME) {
1627 |             return getString();
1628 |         } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1629 |             String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1630 |             if (left == null) {
1631 |               return null;
1632 |             }
1633 |             return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1634 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1635 |              return "this";
1636 |         } else {
1637 |             return null;
1638 | 
1639 |         }
1640 | 
1641 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
1979 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
1980 |     do {
1981 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
1982 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1983 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1984 |         return token;
1985 |       }
1986 |       token = next();
1987 |     } while (true);
1988 |   }

41 |       changeHandler.reportChange();
42 |     }
43 |   }

===== M0050 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 525-532
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
525 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
526 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
527 |     if (typeSet == null) {
528 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
529 |     }
530 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
531 |     typeSet.add(owner);
532 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 605-680
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
605 |   static int precedence(int type) {
606 |     switch (type) {
607 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
608 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
609 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
610 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
611 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
612 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
613 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
614 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
615 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
616 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
617 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
618 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
619 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
620 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
621 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
622 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
623 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
624 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
625 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
626 |       case Token.EQ:
627 |       case Token.NE:
628 |       case Token.SHEQ:
629 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
630 |       case Token.LT:
631 |       case Token.GT:
632 |       case Token.LE:
633 |       case Token.GE:
634 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
635 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
636 |       case Token.LSH:
637 |       case Token.RSH:
638 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
639 |       case Token.SUB:
640 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
641 |       case Token.MUL:
642 |       case Token.MOD:
643 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
644 |       case Token.INC:
645 |       case Token.DEC:
646 |       case Token.NEW:
647 |       case Token.DELPROP:
648 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
649 |       case Token.VOID:
650 |       case Token.NOT:
651 |       case Token.BITNOT:
652 |       case Token.POS:
653 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
656 |       case Token.CALL:
657 |       case Token.EMPTY:
658 |       case Token.FALSE:
659 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
660 |       case Token.GETELEM:
661 |       case Token.GETPROP:
662 |       case Token.GET_REF:
663 |       case Token.IF:
664 |       case Token.LP:
665 |       case Token.NAME:
666 |       case Token.NULL:
667 |       case Token.NUMBER:
668 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
669 |       case Token.REGEXP:
670 |       case Token.RETURN:
671 |       case Token.STRING:
672 |       case Token.THIS:
673 |       case Token.TRUE:
674 |         return 15;
675 | 
676 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
677 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
678 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
679 |     }
680 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hashCode()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 190-207
Documentation:

Method code:
190 |   @Override
191 |   public int hashCode() {
192 |     int hashCode = 0;
193 |     if (returnType != null) {
194 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
195 |     }
196 |     if (parameters != null) {
197 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
198 |       while (param != null) {
199 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
200 |         if (paramType != null) {
201 |           hashCode += paramType.hashCode();
202 |         }
203 |         param = param.getNext();
204 |       }
205 |     }
206 |     return hashCode;
207 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 53-581
Documentation:

Method code:
53 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
54 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
55 |       return;
56 |     }
57 | 
58 |     int type = n.getType();
59 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
60 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
61 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
62 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
63 | 
64 |     // Handle all binary operators
65 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
66 |       Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
67 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
68 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
69 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
70 | 
71 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
72 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
73 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
74 | 
75 |       // Handle associativity.
76 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
77 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
78 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
79 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
80 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
81 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
82 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
83 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
84 |       } else {
85 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
86 |       }
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
91 | 
92 |     switch (type) {
93 |       case Token.TRY: {
94 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
95 |                 first.getNext().getChildCount() <= 1);
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
97 | 
98 |         add("try");
99 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
100 | 
101 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
102 |         // isn't a catch block
103 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
104 |         if (catchblock != null) {
105 |           add(catchblock);
106 |         }
107 | 
108 |         if (childCount == 3) {
109 |           add("finally");
110 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
111 |         }
112 |         break;
113 |       }
114 | 
115 |       case Token.CATCH:
116 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
117 |         if (first.getNext().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
118 |           throw new Error("Catch conditions not suppored because I think" +
119 |                           " that it may be a netscape only feature.");
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         add("catch(");
123 |         add(first);
124 |         add(")");
125 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
126 |         break;
127 | 
128 |       case Token.THROW:
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
130 |         add("throw");
131 |         add(first);
132 | 
133 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
134 |         // parse this.
135 |         cc.endStatement(true);
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.RETURN:
139 |         add("return");
140 |         if (childCount == 1) {
141 |           add(first);
142 |         } else {
143 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
144 |         }
145 |         cc.endStatement();
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.VAR:
149 |         if (first != null) {
150 |           add("var ");
151 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
152 |         }
153 |         break;
154 | 
155 |       case Token.NAME:
156 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
157 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
158 |         } else {
159 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
161 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
162 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
163 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
164 |           } else {
165 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
166 |             // precedence.
167 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
168 |           }
169 |         }
170 | 
171 |         break;
172 | 
173 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
174 |         add("[");
175 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
176 |         add("]");
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.LP:
180 |         add("(");
181 |         addList(first);
182 |         add(")");
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.COMMA:
186 |         addList(first, false, context);
187 |         break;
188 | 
189 |       case Token.NUMBER:
190 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
191 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
192 |         break;
193 | 
194 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
195 |       case Token.VOID:
196 |       case Token.NOT:
197 |       case Token.BITNOT:
198 |       case Token.POS:
199 |       case Token.NEG: {
200 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
201 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
202 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
203 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
204 |         break;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       case Token.HOOK: {
208 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
209 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
210 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
211 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
212 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), p);
213 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
214 |         addExpr(last, p);
215 |         break;
216 |       }
217 | 
218 |       case Token.REGEXP:
219 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
220 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
221 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
222 |         }
223 | 
224 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString());
225 | 
226 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
227 |         if (childCount == 2) {
228 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
229 |         } else {
230 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
231 |           add(regexp);
232 |         }
233 |         break;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.GET_REF:
236 |         add(first);
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
241 |         add(first);
242 |         add(".");
243 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
244 |         break;
245 | 
246 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
247 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
248 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
249 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
250 |           add("(");
251 |         }
252 | 
253 |         add("function");
254 |         add(first);
255 | 
256 |         add(first.getNext());
257 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
258 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
259 | 
260 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
261 |           add(")");
262 |         }
263 |         break;
264 | 
265 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
266 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
267 |         boolean stripBlock = n.isSyntheticBlock() ||
268 |             ((context != Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK) && (n.getChildCount() < 2));
269 |         if (!stripBlock) {
270 |           cc.beginBlock();
271 |         }
272 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
273 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
274 | 
275 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
276 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
277 |             cc.endStatement();
278 |           }
279 | 
280 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
281 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
282 |           }
283 | 
284 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
285 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
286 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
287 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
288 |           }
289 |         }
290 |         if (!stripBlock) {
291 |           cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
292 |         }
293 |         break;
294 |       }
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FOR:
297 |         if (childCount == 4) {
298 |           add("for(");
299 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
300 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
301 |           } else {
302 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
303 |           }
304 |           add(";");
305 |           add(first.getNext());
306 |           add(";");
307 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
308 |           add(")");
309 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
310 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
311 |         } else {
312 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
313 |           add("for(");
314 |           add(first);
315 |           add("in");
316 |           add(first.getNext());
317 |           add(")");
318 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
319 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
320 |         }
321 |         break;
322 | 
323 |       case Token.DO:
324 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
325 |         add("do");
326 |         addNonEmptyExpression(first, Context.OTHER, false);
327 |         add("while(");
328 |         add(last);
329 |         add(")");
330 |         cc.endStatement();
331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.WHILE:
334 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
335 |         add("while(");
336 |         add(first);
337 |         add(")");
338 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
339 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.EMPTY:
343 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
344 |         break;
345 | 
346 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
347 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
348 |         Preconditions.checkState(last.getType() == Token.STRING);
349 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
350 |         if (needsParens) {
351 |           add("(");
352 |         }
353 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
354 |         if (needsParens) {
355 |           add(")");
356 |         }
357 |         add(".");
358 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
359 |         break;
360 |       }
361 | 
362 |       case Token.GETELEM:
363 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
364 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
365 |         add("[");
366 |         add(first.getNext());
367 |         add("]");
368 |         break;
369 | 
370 |       case Token.WITH:
371 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
372 |         add("with(");
373 |         add(first);
374 |         add(")");
375 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
376 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.INC:
380 |       case Token.DEC: {
381 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
382 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
383 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
384 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
385 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
386 |         if (postProp != 0) {
387 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
388 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
389 |         } else {
390 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
391 |           add(first);
392 |         }
393 |         break;
394 |       }
395 | 
396 |       case Token.CALL:
397 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
398 |         add("(");
399 |         addList(first.getNext());
400 |         add(")");
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.IF:
404 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
405 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
406 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
407 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
408 |           cc.beginBlock();
409 |         }
410 | 
411 |         add("if(");
412 |         add(first);
413 |         add(")");
414 | 
415 |         if (hasElse) {
416 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
417 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
418 |           add("else");
419 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
420 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
421 |         } else {
422 |           addNonEmptyExpression(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
423 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
424 |         }
425 | 
426 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
427 |           cc.endBlock();
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.NULL:
432 |       case Token.THIS:
433 |       case Token.FALSE:
434 |       case Token.TRUE:
435 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
436 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
441 |         add("continue");
442 |         if (childCount == 1) {
443 |           add(" ");
444 |           add(first);
445 |         }
446 |         cc.endStatement();
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
451 |         add("debugger");
452 |         cc.endStatement();
453 |         break;
454 | 
455 |       case Token.BREAK:
456 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
457 |         add("break");
458 |         if (childCount == 1) {
459 |           add(" ");
460 |           add(first);
461 |         }
462 |         cc.endStatement();
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
466 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Enable this exception once the external users of
467 |         //     CodePrinter have been corrected.
468 |         // throw new Error("EXPR_VOID should not be used in this codebase.");
469 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
470 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
471 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
472 |         cc.endStatement();
473 |         break;
474 | 
475 |       case Token.NEW:
476 |         add("new ");
477 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
478 | 
479 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
480 |         // to force parens. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
481 |         // first viable parens
482 |         if (NodeUtil.containsCall(first)) {
483 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
484 |         }
485 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
486 | 
487 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
488 |         Node next = first.getNext();
489 |         if (next != null) {
490 |           add("(");
491 |           addList(next);
492 |           add(")");
493 |         }
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.STRING:
497 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
498 |         add(jsString(n.getString()));
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.DELPROP:
502 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
503 |         add("delete ");
504 |         add(first);
505 |         break;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
508 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
509 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
510 |         if (needsParens) {
511 |           add("(");
512 |         }
513 |         add("{");
514 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
515 |           if (c != first) {
516 |             cc.listSeparator();
517 |           }
518 | 
519 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
520 |           // not JavaScript keywords
521 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
522 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
523 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
524 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
525 |               // unicode escaped.
526 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
527 |             add(c.getString());
528 |           } else {
529 |             addExpr(c, 1);
530 |           }
531 |           add(":");
532 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
533 |         }
534 |         add("}");
535 |         if (needsParens) {
536 |           add(")");
537 |         }
538 |         break;
539 |       }
540 | 
541 |       case Token.SWITCH:
542 |         add("switch(");
543 |         add(first);
544 |         add(")");
545 |         cc.beginBlock();
546 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
547 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.CASE:
551 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
552 |         add("case ");
553 |         add(first);
554 |         addCaseBody(last);
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
558 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
559 |         add("default");
560 |         addCaseBody(first);
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.LABEL:
564 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
565 |         add(first);
566 |         add(":");
567 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
568 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
572 |       // ignored for our purposes.
573 |       case Token.SETNAME:
574 |         break;
575 | 
576 |       default:
577 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
578 |     }
579 | 
580 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
581 |   }

/**
   * Flattens a particular prefix of a single name reference.
   *
   * @param alias A flattened prefix name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param n The node corresponding to a subproperty name (e.g. "a.b.c.d")
   * @param depth The difference in depth between the property name and

Method code:
393 |   private void flattenNameRefAtDepth(String alias, Node n, int depth,

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java

/**
   * Runs through all namespaces (prefixes of classes and enums), and checks if
   * any of them have been used in an unsafe way.
   */
Method code:
246 |   private void checkNamespaces() {
247 |     for (Name name : nameMap.values()) {
248 |       if (name.isNamespace() && name.refs != null &&
249 |           (name.aliasingGets > 0 || name.localSets + name.globalSets > 1)) {
250 |         boolean initialized = name.declaration != null;
251 |         for (Ref ref : name.refs) {
252 |           if (ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL ||
253 |               ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_LOCAL) {
254 |             if (initialized) {
255 |               warnAboutNamespaceRedefinition(name, ref);
256 |             }
257 | 
258 |             initialized = true;
259 |           } else if (ref.type == Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET) {
260 |             warnAboutNamespaceAliasing(name, ref);
261 |           }
262 |         }
263 |       }
264 |     }
265 |   }

   * Add blocks to IF, WHILE, DO, etc.

117 |   private void normalizeBlocks(Node n) {
118 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n)
119 |         && n.getType() != Token.LABEL
120 |         && n.getType() != Token.SWITCH) {
121 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 106-153
Documentation:

Method code:
106 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
107 |                                    String sourceString,
108 |                                    Config config,
109 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
110 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
111 |         config, errorReporter);
112 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
113 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
114 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
115 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
116 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
117 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
118 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
119 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
120 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
121 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
122 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
123 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
124 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC &&
125 |             (comment.getValue().contains("@fileoverview") ||
126 |              comment.getValue().contains("@preserve") ||
127 |              comment.getValue().contains("@license"))) {
128 |           JSDocInfo info = irFactory.parseJSDocInfo(comment.getValue(),
129 |               comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
130 |               fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
131 |           if (info != null && fileOverviewInfo == null) {
132 |             fileOverviewInfo = info;
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |       }
136 | 
137 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
138 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
139 |       // to that node.
140 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
141 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
142 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
143 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
144 |         }
145 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       Comment[] comments = new Comment[node.getComments().size()];
149 |       comments = node.getComments().toArray(comments);
150 |       irFactory.parseAllJsDocInfo(irNode, comments, 0);
151 |     }
152 |     return irNode;
153 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
973 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
974 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
975 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
976 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
977 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
978 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
979 |       } else {
980 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
981 |       }
982 |     }
983 |     return builder.build();
984 |   }

===== M0109 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 155-177
Documentation:

Method code:
155 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
156 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
157 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
158 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
159 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
160 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
161 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
162 |     } else {
163 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
164 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
165 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
166 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
167 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
168 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
169 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
170 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 |     if (node.getJsDoc() != null) {
174 |       irNode.putProp(TMP_JSDOC_PROP, node.getJsDoc());
175 |     }
176 |     return irNode;
177 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 85-108
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr, compiler.isIdeMode(),
90 |           compiler.getTypeRegistry(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(Compiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.normalizeNodeTypes(root);
102 |       compiler.annotateCodingConvention(root);
103 |     }
104 | 
105 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
106 |     // the source file and module.
107 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
108 |   }

119 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

134 |       case Token.LSH:

136 |       case Token.MOD:
137 |       case Token.MUL:
138 |       case Token.NE:
139 |       case Token.OR:

143 |       case Token.SUB:
144 |       case Token.URSH:
145 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
146 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
147 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:

157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
160 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
161 |       case Token.BITNOT:
162 |       case Token.DEC:
163 |       case Token.DELPROP:
164 |       case Token.INC:
165 |       case Token.NEG:
166 |       case Token.NOT:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
169 |       case Token.VOID:
170 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
171 |       case Token.BLOCK:
172 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
173 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
174 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
175 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
176 |         } else {
177 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
178 |                                           node.getClass() +
179 |                                           " type: " +
180 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
181 |         }
182 |       case Token.BREAK:
183 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
184 |       case Token.CALL:
185 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
186 |       case Token.CASE:
187 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
188 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.FINALLY:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
200 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
201 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
202 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
203 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
204 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
205 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
206 |         } else {
207 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
208 |                                           node.getClass() +
209 |                                           " type: " +
210 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
211 |         }
212 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
213 |       case Token.FALSE:
214 |       case Token.NULL:
215 |       case Token.THIS:
216 |       case Token.TRUE:
217 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
218 |       case Token.FOR:
219 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
220 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
221 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
222 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
223 |         } else {
224 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
225 |                                           node.getClass() +
226 |                                           " type: " +
227 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
228 |         }
229 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
230 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
231 |       case Token.GETELEM:
232 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
233 |       case Token.GETPROP:
234 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
235 |       case Token.HOOK:
236 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
237 |       case Token.IF:
238 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
239 |       case Token.LABEL:
240 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
241 |       case Token.LP:
242 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
243 |       case Token.NAME:
244 |         return processName((Name) node);
245 |       case Token.NEW:
246 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
247 |       case Token.NUMBER:
248 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
250 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.REGEXP:
252 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.RETURN:
254 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
255 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
256 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
257 |       case Token.STRING:
258 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
259 |       case Token.SWITCH:
260 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.THROW:
262 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.TRY:
264 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(Name,String,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 811-840
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds global variable "stubs" for any properties of a global name that are
   * only set in a local scope or read but never set.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name of the object whose properties we are
   *     adding stubs for (e.g. "a$b$c")
   * @param parent The node to which new global variables should be added
   *     as children
   * @param addAfter The child of after which new
   *     variables should be added (may be null)
   * @return The number of variables added
   */
Method code:
811 |   private int addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(
812 |       Name n, String alias, Node parent, Node addAfter) {
813 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent));
814 |     int numStubs = 0;
815 |     if (n.props != null) {
816 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
817 |         if (p.needsToBeStubbed()) {
818 |           String propAlias = alias + '$' + p.name;
819 |           Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
820 |           Node newVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
821 |           if (addAfter == null) {
822 |             parent.addChildToFront(newVar);
823 |           } else {
824 |             parent.addChildAfter(newVar, addAfter);
825 |             addAfter = newVar;
826 |           }
827 |           numStubs++;
828 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
829 | 
830 |           // Determine if this is a constant var by checking the first
831 |           // reference to it. Don't check the declaration, as it might be null.
832 |           if (p.refs.get(0).node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(
833 |                 Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
834 |             nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |       }
838 |     }
839 |     return numStubs;
840 |   }

/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")
     * @return The {@link Name} instance for {@code name}
     */
Method code:
729 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {
730 |       Name node = nameMap.get(name);
731 |       if (node == null) {

733 |         if (i >= 0) {
734 |           String parentName = name.substring(0, i);
735 |           Name parent = getOrCreateName(parentName);

737 |         } else {
738 |           node = new Name(name, null, inExterns);
739 |           globalNames.add(node);

742 |       }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.declareVarsForObjLitValues(String,Node,Node,Node,Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 721-796
Documentation:
/**
   * Declares global variables to serve as aliases for the values in an object
   * literal, optionally removing all of the object literal's keys and values.
   *
   * @param alias The object literal's flattened name (e.g. "a$b$c")
   * @param objlit The OBJLIT node
   * @param varNode The VAR node to which new global variables should be added
   *     as children
   * @param nameToAddAfter The child of {@code varNode} after which new
   *     variables should be added (may be null)
   * @param varParent {@code varNode}'s parent
   * @param discardKeys Whether to eliminate the object literal's keys after
   *     declaring variables for its values
   * @return The number of variables added
   */
Method code:
721 |   private int declareVarsForObjLitValues(
722 |       String alias, Node objlit, Node varNode, Node nameToAddAfter,
723 |       Node varParent, boolean discardKeys) {
724 |     int numVars = 0;
725 |     int arbitraryNameCounter = 0;
726 | 
727 |     for (Node key = objlit.getFirstChild(), nextKey; key != null;
728 |          key = nextKey) {
729 |       Node value = key.getNext();
730 |       nextKey = value.getNext();
731 | 
732 |       // We generate arbitrary names for keys that aren't valid JavaScript
733 |       // identifiers, since those keys are never referenced. (If they were,
734 |       // this object literal's child names wouldn't be collapsible.) The only
735 |       // reason that we don't eliminate them entirely is the off chance that
736 |       // their values are expressions that have side effects.
737 |       boolean isJsIdentifier = key.getType() != Token.NUMBER &&
738 |                                TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key.getString());
739 |       String propAlias = alias + '$' +
740 |           (isJsIdentifier ? key.getString()
741 |               : String.valueOf(++arbitraryNameCounter));
742 |       String qName = propAlias.replace('$', '.');
743 | 
744 |       Node refNode = null;
745 |       if (discardKeys) {
746 |         objlit.removeChild(key);
747 |         objlit.removeChild(value);
748 |       } else {
749 |         // Substitute a reference for the value.
750 |         refNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
751 |         if (key.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
752 |           refNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
753 |         }
754 |         objlit.replaceChildAfter(key, refNode);
755 |       }
756 | 
757 |       // Declare the collapsed name as a variable with the original value.
758 |       Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
759 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(value);
760 |       if (key.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
761 |         nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
762 |       }
763 |       Node newVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
764 |       if (nameToAddAfter != null) {
765 |         varParent.addChildAfter(newVar, nameToAddAfter);
766 |       } else {
767 |         varParent.addChildBefore(newVar, varNode);
768 |       }
769 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
770 |       nameToAddAfter = newVar;
771 | 
772 |       if (isJsIdentifier) {
773 |         // Update the global name's node ancestry if it hasn't already been
774 |         // done. (Duplicate keys in an object literal can bring us here twice
775 |         // for the same global name.)
776 |         Name p = nameMap.get(qName);
777 |         if (p != null) {
778 |           if (!discardKeys) {
779 |             Ref newAlias =
780 |                 p.declaration.cloneAndReclassify(Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET);
781 |             newAlias.node = refNode;
782 |             p.addRef(newAlias);
783 |           }
784 | 
785 |           p.declaration.node = nameNode;
786 | 
787 |           if (value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
788 |             checkForHosedThisReferences(value, value.getJSDocInfo(), p);
789 |           }
790 |         }
791 |       }
792 | 
793 |       numVars++;
794 |     }
795 |     return numVars;
796 |   }

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodingConventionAnnotator.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodingConventionAnnotator.java
Original range: 52-96
Documentation:

Method code:
52 |   @Override
53 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
54 |     switch (n.getType()) {
55 |       case Token.NAME:
56 |       case Token.STRING:
57 |         if (convention.isConstant(n.getString())) {
58 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
59 |         }
60 |         break;
61 | 
62 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
63 |         JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
64 |         if (fnInfo == null) {
65 |           // Look for the info on other nodes.
66 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
67 |             // on ASSIGNs
68 |             fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
69 |           } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
70 |             // on var NAME = function() { ... };
71 |             fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
72 |           }
73 |         }
74 | 
75 |         // Compute which function parameters are optional and
76 |         // which are var_args.
77 |         Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
78 |         for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
79 |              arg != null;
80 |              arg = arg.getNext()) {
81 |           String argName = arg.getString();
82 |           JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
83 |               null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
84 | 
85 |           if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
86 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
87 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
88 |           }
89 |           if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
90 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
91 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
92 |           }
93 |         }
94 |         break;
95 |     }
96 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isControlStructureCodeBlock(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 898-922
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is code node for FOR, DO,
   * WHILE, WITH, or IF node.
   */
Method code:
898 |   static boolean isControlStructureCodeBlock(Node parent, Node n) {
899 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
900 |       case Token.FOR:
901 |       case Token.WHILE:
902 |       case Token.LABEL:
903 |       case Token.WITH:
904 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
905 |       case Token.DO:
906 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
907 |       case Token.IF:
908 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != n;
909 |       case Token.TRY:
910 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n || parent.getLastChild() == n;
911 |       case Token.CATCH:
912 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
913 |       case Token.SWITCH:
914 |       case Token.CASE:
915 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != n;
916 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
917 |         return true;
918 |       default:
919 |         Preconditions.checkState(isControlStructure(parent));
920 |         return false;
921 |     }
922 |   }

===== M0176 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 569-624
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name
   * that occurs at an ASSIGN node. See comment for
   * {@link #updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration}.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n} (e.g. "a", "a$b$c")
   */
Method code:
569 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(
570 |       Name n, String alias) {
571 |     // NOTE: It's important that we don't add additional nodes
572 |     // (e.g. a var node before the exprstmt) because the exprstmt might be
573 |     // the child of an if statement that's not inside a block).
574 | 
575 |     Ref ref = n.declaration;
576 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getNext();
577 |     Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR);
578 |     Node varParent = ref.node.getAncestor(3);
579 |     Node gramps = ref.node.getAncestor(2);
580 |     boolean isObjLit = rvalue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT;
581 | 
582 |     if (isObjLit && n.canEliminate()) {
583 |       // Eliminate the object literal altogether.
584 |       varParent.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
585 |       ref.node = null;
586 | 
587 |     } else {
588 |       if (rvalue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
589 |         checkForHosedThisReferences(rvalue, n.docInfo, n);
590 |       }
591 | 
592 |       ref.node.getParent().removeChild(rvalue);
593 | 
594 |       Node nameNode = NodeUtil.newName(
595 |           alias, ref.node.getAncestor(2), n.fullName());
596 | 
597 |       if (ref.node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
598 |         nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
599 |       }
600 | 
601 |       varNode.addChildToBack(nameNode);
602 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(rvalue);
603 |       varParent.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
604 | 
605 |       // Update the node ancestry stored in the reference.
606 |       ref.node = nameNode;
607 |     }
608 | 
609 |     if (isObjLit) {
610 |       boolean discardKeys = n.aliasingGets == 0;
611 |       declareVarsForObjLitValues(
612 |           alias, rvalue,
613 |           varNode, varParent.getChildBefore(varNode), varParent,
614 |           discardKeys);
615 |     }
616 | 
617 |     addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(n, alias, varParent, varNode);
618 | 
619 |     if (!varNode.hasChildren()) {
620 |       varParent.removeChild(varNode);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
624 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

Documentation:
/**
     * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
     * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
     * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
     * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
     */
Method code:
1102 |     protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1103 |       if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1104 |         return -1;
1105 |       } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1106 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1107 |       } else {
1108 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1109 |       }
1110 |     }

/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name
   * that occurs at a VAR node. See comment for
   * {@link #updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration}.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a")
   */
Method code:
656 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtVarNode(Name n) {
657 |     Ref ref = n.declaration;
658 |     String name = ref.node.getString();
659 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getFirstChild();
660 |     Node varNode = ref.node.getParent();
661 |     Node gramps = varNode.getParent();
662 | 
663 |     boolean isObjLit = rvalue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT;
664 |     int numChanges = 0;
665 | 
666 |     if (isObjLit) {
667 |       boolean discardKeys = n.aliasingGets == 0;
668 |       numChanges += declareVarsForObjLitValues(
669 |           name, rvalue, varNode, gramps.getChildBefore(varNode),

671 |     }

676 |       varNode.removeChild(ref.node);

683 |       // parse tree.
684 |       ref.node = null;
685 |     }

688 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
689 |     }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 440-461
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapses definitions of the collapsible properties of a global name.
   * Recurses on subnames that also represent JavaScript objects with
   * collapsible properties.
   *
   * @param n A node representing a global name
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n}
   */
Method code:
440 |   private void collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(Name n, String alias) {
441 |     boolean canCollapseChildNames = n.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames();
442 | 
443 |     // Handle this name first so that nested object literals get unrolled.
444 |     if (n.canCollapse() && canCollapseChildNames) {
445 |       updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(n, alias);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     if (n.props != null) {
449 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
450 |         // Recurse first so that saved node ancestries are intact when needed.
451 |         collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(p, alias + '$' + p.name);
452 | 
453 |         if (!p.inExterns && canCollapseChildNames && p.declaration != null &&
454 |             p.declaration.node != null &&
455 |             p.declaration.node.getParent() != null &&
456 |             p.declaration.node.getParent().getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
457 |           updateSimpleDeclaration(alias + '$' + p.name, p.declaration);
458 |         }
459 |       }
460 |     }
461 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1148-1150
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an anonymous function? An anonymous function is one that
   * has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of anonymous functions:
   * <pre>
   * function () {}
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> anonymous:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A FUNCTION node
   * @return Whether n is an anonymous function
   */
Method code:
1148 |   static boolean isFunctionAnonymous(Node n) {
1149 |     return !isStatement(n);
1150 |   }

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1765-1779
Documentation:
/**
     * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
     */
Method code:
1765 |     public Node cloneTree() {
1766 |         Node result = cloneNode();
1767 |         for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1768 |             Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1769 |             n2clone.parent = result;
1770 |             if (result.last != null) {
1771 |                 result.last.next = n2clone;
1772 |             }
1773 |             if (result.first == null) {
1774 |                 result.first = n2clone;
1775 |             }
1776 |             result.last = n2clone;
1777 |         }
1778 |         return result;
1779 |     }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 231-259
Documentation:

Method code:
231 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
232 |     maybeEndStatement();
233 | 
234 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
235 |     char prev = getLastChar();
236 | 
237 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
238 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
239 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
240 |       append(" ");
241 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
242 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
243 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
244 |       append(" ");
245 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
246 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
247 |       append(" ");
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
251 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
252 | 
253 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
254 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
255 |     // because it looks strange.
256 |     if (binOp) {
257 |       maybeCutLine();
258 |     }
259 |   }

795 |     private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
796 |         int count = 0;
797 |         for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
798 |             count++;

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
566 |   public Scope getScope() {

===== M0255 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.flattenPrefixes(String,Name,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 357-382
Documentation:
/**
   * Flattens all occurrences of a name as a prefix of subnames beginning
   * with a particular subname.
   *
   * @param n A global property name (e.g. "a.b.c.d")
   * @param alias A flattened prefix name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param depth The difference in depth between the property name and
   *    the prefix name (e.g. 2)
   */
Method code:
357 |   private void flattenPrefixes(String alias, Name n, int depth) {
358 |     // Only flatten the prefix of a name declaration if the name being
359 |     // initialized is fully qualified (i.e. not an object literal key).
360 |     String originalName = n.fullName();
361 |     if (n.declaration != null && n.declaration.node != null &&
362 |         n.declaration.node.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
363 |       flattenNameRefAtDepth(alias, n.declaration.node, depth, originalName);
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     if (n.refs != null) {
367 |       for (Ref r : n.refs) {
368 | 
369 |         // References inside a complex assign (a = x.y = 0)
370 |         // have twins. We should only flatten one of the twins.
371 |         if (r.getTwin() == null || r.isSet()) {
372 |           flattenNameRefAtDepth(alias, r.node, depth, originalName);
373 |         }
374 |       }
375 |     }
376 | 
377 |     if (n.props != null) {
378 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
379 |         flattenPrefixes(alias, p, depth + 1);
380 |       }
381 |     }
382 |   }

   * Creates a map to make looking up an input by name fast. Also checks for
   * duplicate inputs.

379 |   void initInputsByNameMap() {
380 |     inputsByName_ = new HashMap<String, CompilerInput>();
381 |     for (CompilerInput input : externs_) {
382 |       String name = input.getName();

387 |       }
