Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 60-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
60 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
61 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
62 |     int c;
63 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
64 |     for (;;) {
65 |       // eat white spaces
66 |       for (;;) {
67 |         charno = -1;
68 |         c = getChar();
69 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
71 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
72 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
73 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
74 |           break;
75 |         }
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       switch (c) {
79 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
80 |         case '@':
81 |           do {
82 |             c = getChar();
83 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
84 |               addToString(c);
85 |             } else {
86 |               ungetChar(c);
87 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
88 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
89 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
90 |             }
91 |           } while (true);
92 | 
93 |         case '*':
94 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
95 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
96 |           } else {
97 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |         case ',':
101 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
102 | 
103 |         case '>':
104 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
105 | 
106 |         case '(':
107 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
108 | 
109 |         case ')':
110 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
111 | 
112 |         case '{':
113 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
114 | 
115 |         case '}':
116 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
117 | 
118 |         case '[':
119 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
120 | 
121 |         case ']':
122 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
123 | 
124 |         case '?':
125 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
126 | 
127 |         case '!':
128 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
129 | 
130 |         case ':':
131 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
132 | 
133 |         case '=':
134 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
135 | 
136 |         case '|':
137 |           matchChar('|');
138 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
139 | 
140 |         case '.':
141 |           c = getChar();
142 |           if (c == '<') {
143 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
144 |           } else {
145 |             if (c == '.') {
146 |               c = getChar();
147 |               if (c == '.') {
148 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
149 |               } else {
150 |                 addToString('.');
151 |               }
152 |             }
153 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
154 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
155 |             c = '.';
156 |           }
157 |           // fall through
158 | 
159 |         default: {
160 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
161 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
162 |           int c1 = c;
163 |           addToString(c);
164 |           int c2 = getChar();
165 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
166 |             ungetChar(c2);
167 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
168 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
169 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
170 |           } else {
171 |             do {
172 |               c1 = c2;
173 |               c2 = getChar();
174 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
175 |                 ungetChar(c2);
176 |                 ungetChar(c1);
177 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
178 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
179 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
180 |               } else {
181 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                 } else {
184 |                   ungetChar(c2);
185 |                   addToString(c1);
186 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
187 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
188 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
189 |                 }
190 |               }
191 |             } while (true);
192 |           }
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTypeNormalizer.normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTypeNormalizer.java
Original range: 68-84
Documentation:
/**
   * Normalize where JSDoc annotations appear on the AST.
   *
   * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
   * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
   * value. For example,
   * <pre>
   * var x = {
   *   / JSDOC /
   *   a: 'b',
   *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
   * };
   * </pre>
   *
   * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
   * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private void normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node n) {
69 |     if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
70 |       for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
71 |            key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
72 |         Node value = key.getNext();
73 |         if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
74 |             key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
75 |           value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
76 |         }
77 |       }
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
81 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
82 |       normalizeJsDocAnnotations(child);
83 |     }
84 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 410-432
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
410 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
411 |       switch (n.getType()) {
412 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
413 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
414 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
415 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
416 |         case Token.OR:
417 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
418 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
419 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
420 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
421 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
422 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
423 |         case Token.HOOK:
424 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
425 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
426 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
427 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
428 |           Node third = second.getNext();
429 |           return getValueType(third);
430 |       }
431 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
432 |     }

/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name.
   * This involves flattening the global name (if it's not just a global
   * variable name already), collapsing object literal keys into global
   * variables, declaring stub global variables for properties added later
   * in a local scope, and eliminating the global name entirely (if possible).
   *

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 394-438
Documentation:

Method code:
394 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
395 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
396 |       cursor++;
397 |       --ungetCursor;
398 |       if (charno == -1) {
399 |         charno = getOffset();
400 |       }
401 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     for(;;) {
405 |       int c;
406 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
407 |         hitEOF = true;
408 |         if (charno == -1) {
409 |           charno = getOffset();
410 |         }
411 |         return EOF_CHAR;
412 |       }
413 |       cursor++;
414 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
415 | 
416 | 
417 |       if (c <= 127) {
418 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
419 |           lineEndChar = c;
420 |           c = '\n';
421 |         }
422 |       } else {
423 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
424 |           continue;
425 |         }
426 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
427 |           lineEndChar = c;
428 |           c = '\n';
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       if (charno == -1) {
433 |         charno = getOffset();
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       return c;
437 |     }
438 |   }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 75-105
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
76 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
77 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
78 | 
79 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
80 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
81 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
82 | 
83 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
84 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
85 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
86 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n)) {
87 |         declareVar(fnName, fnNameNode, n, null, null, n);
88 |       }
89 | 
90 |       // Args: Declare function variables
91 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
92 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
93 |            a = a.getNext()) {
94 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
95 |         declareVar(a.getString(), a, args, n, null, n);
96 |       }
97 | 
98 |       // Body
99 |       scanVars(body, n);
100 |     } else {
101 |       // It's the global block
102 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
103 |       scanVars(n, null);
104 |     }
105 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 234-326
Documentation:
/** {@inheritDoc} */
Method code:
234 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
236 |         return;
237 |       }
238 | 
239 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
240 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
241 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
242 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
243 |       }
244 | 
245 |       String name;
246 |       boolean isSet = false;
247 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
248 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
249 | 
250 |       switch (n.getType()) {
251 |         case Token.STRING:
252 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
253 |           name = null;
254 |           if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
255 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
256 |           }
257 |           if (name == null) return;
258 |           isSet = true;
259 |           type = getValueType(n.getNext());
260 |           break;
261 |         case Token.NAME:
262 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
263 |           if (parent != null) {
264 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
265 |               case Token.VAR:
266 |                 isSet = true;
267 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
268 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
269 |                 break;
270 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
271 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
272 |                   isSet = true;
273 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
274 |                 }
275 |                 break;
276 |               case Token.GETPROP:
277 |                 return;
278 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
279 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
280 |                 if (gramps == null ||
281 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(parent)) return;
282 |                 isSet = true;
283 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
284 |                 break;
285 |             }
286 |           }
287 |           name = n.getString();
288 |           break;
289 |         case Token.GETPROP:
290 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
291 |           if (parent != null) {
292 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
293 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
294 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
295 |                   isSet = true;
296 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
297 |                   isPropAssign = true;
298 |                 }
299 |                 break;
300 |               case Token.GETPROP:
301 |                 return;
302 |             }
303 |           }
304 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
305 |           if (name == null) return;
306 |           break;
307 |         default:
308 |           return;
309 |       }
310 | 
311 |       // We are only interested in global names.
312 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
313 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
314 |         return;
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       if (isSet) {
318 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
319 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
320 |         } else {
321 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
322 |         }
323 |       } else {
324 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
325 |       }
326 |     }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
311 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
312 |     if (c <= 127) {
313 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
314 |     } else {
315 |       return c == 0xA0
316 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }

Original range: 1267-1312
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1267 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1268 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1269 | 
1270 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1271 | 
1272 |     do {
1273 |       switch (token) {
1274 |         case ANNOTATION:
1275 |         case EOC:
1276 |         case EOF:
1277 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1278 | 
1279 |         case STAR:
1280 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1281 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1282 |               builder.append(' ');
1283 |             }
1284 | 
1285 |             builder.append('*');
1286 |           }
1287 | 
1288 |           token = next();
1289 |           continue;
1290 | 
1291 |         case EOL:
1292 |           ignoreStar = true;
1293 |           builder.append('\n');
1294 |           token = next();
1295 |           continue;
1296 | 
1297 |         default:
1298 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1299 |             builder.append(' ');
1300 |           }
1301 | 
1302 |           ignoreStar = false;
1303 | 
1304 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1305 | 
1306 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1307 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1308 |           builder.append(line);
1309 |           token = next();
1310 |       }
1311 |     } while (true);
1312 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-516
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean anonymous = parent != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n);
490 | 
491 |     if (!anonymous) {
492 |       // Named functions are parent of the containing scope.
493 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     curNode = n;
497 |     pushScope(n);
498 | 
499 |     if (anonymous) {
500 |       // Anonymous function names are parent of the contained scope.
501 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
505 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
506 | 
507 |     // Args
508 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
509 | 
510 |     // Body
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
512 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
513 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
514 | 
515 |     popScope();
516 |   }

732 |     {
733 |         PropListItem x = propListHead;

735 |             PropListItem prev = null;
736 |             while (x.type != propType) {

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateSimpleDeclaration(String,Ref)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 473-535
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the initial assignment to a collapsible property at global scope
   * by changing it to a variable declaration (e.g. a.b = 1 -> var a$b = 1).
   * The property's value may either be a primitive or an object literal or
   * function whose properties aren't collapsible.
   *
   * @param alias The flattened property name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param ref An object containing information about the assignment getting
   *     updated
   */
Method code:
473 |   private void updateSimpleDeclaration(String alias, Ref ref) {
474 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getNext();
475 |     Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
476 |     Node gramps = parent.getParent();
477 |     Node greatGramps = gramps.getParent();
478 |     Node greatGreatGramps = greatGramps.getParent();
479 | 
480 |     // Create the new alias node.
481 |     Node nameNode = NodeUtil.newName(alias, gramps.getFirstChild(),
482 |         alias.replace("$", "."));
483 |     if (ref.node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
484 |       nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     if (gramps.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
488 |       // BEFORE: a.b.c = ...;
489 |       //   exprstmt
490 |       //     assign
491 |       //       getprop
492 |       //         getprop
493 |       //           name a
494 |       //           string b
495 |       //         string c
496 |       //       NODE
497 |       // AFTER: var a$b$c = ...;
498 |       //   var
499 |       //     name a$b$c
500 |       //       NODE
501 | 
502 |       // Remove the rvalue (NODE).
503 |       parent.removeChild(rvalue);
504 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(rvalue);
505 | 
506 |       Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
507 |       greatGramps.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
508 |     } else {
509 |       // This must be a complex assignment.
510 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(ref.getTwin());
511 | 
512 |       // BEFORE:
513 |       // ... (x.y = 3);
514 |       //
515 |       // AFTER:
516 |       // var x$y;
517 |       // ... (x$y = 3);
518 | 
519 |       Node current = gramps;
520 |       Node currentParent = gramps.getParent();
521 |       for (; currentParent.getType() != Token.SCRIPT &&
522 |              currentParent.getType() != Token.BLOCK;
523 |            current = currentParent,
524 |            currentParent = currentParent.getParent()) {}
525 | 
526 |       // Create a stub variable declaration right
527 |       // before the current statement.
528 |       Node stubVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode.cloneTree());
529 |       currentParent.addChildBefore(stubVar, current);
530 | 
531 |       parent.replaceChild(ref.node, nameNode);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
535 |   }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties

Method code:

480 |          */

482 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
483 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
484 |     }

===== M0389 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 110-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
110 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
111 |     switch (n.getType()) {
112 |       case Token.VAR:
113 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
114 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
115 |              child != null;) {
116 |           Node next = child.getNext();
117 |           Preconditions.checkState(child.getType() == Token.NAME);
118 | 
119 |           String name = child.getString();
120 |           declareVar(name, child, n, parent, null, n);
121 |           child = next;
122 |         }
123 |         return;
124 | 
125 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
126 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n)) {
127 |           return;
128 |         }
129 | 
130 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
132 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
133 |           return;
134 |         }
135 |         declareVar(fnName, n.getFirstChild(), n, parent, null, n);
136 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
137 | 
138 |       case Token.CATCH:
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
140 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
141 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
142 |         // is the code block
143 | 
144 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
145 |         final Node block = var.getNext().getNext();
146 | 
147 |         declareVar(var.getString(), var, n, parent, null, n);
148 |         scanVars(block, n);
149 |         return;  // only one child to scan
150 | 
151 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
152 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child, n);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 571-593
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
571 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
572 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
573 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap) {
574 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
575 |     CodeConsumer cp =
576 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
577 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
578 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap)
579 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap);
580 |     CodeGenerator cg =
581 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
582 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(cp)
583 |         : new CodeGenerator(cp);
584 |     cg.add(root);
585 | 
586 |     String code = ((HasGetCode) cp).getCode();
587 | 
588 |     if (createSourceMap) {
589 |       cp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
590 |     }
591 | 
592 |     return code;
593 |   }

   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and

   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param sourceName The source file containing the JSDoc.

113 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
114 |       return built;
115 |     } else {
116 |       return null;
117 |     }
118 |   }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 484-533
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |    @Override
485 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
486 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
487 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
488 |       if (name == null) {
489 |         name = new Name();
490 |         name.setIdentifier("");
491 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
492 |       }
493 |       Node node = new com.google.javascript.rhino.FunctionNode(
494 |           name.getIdentifier());
495 |       node.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, functionNode.getSourceName());
496 |       Node newName = transform(name);
497 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
498 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
499 |         // declaration.
500 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
501 |         // TODO(user) Mark line number of paren correctly.
502 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
503 |         // same line as the function keyword.
504 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
505 |             functionNode.getLp();
506 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
510 |       Node lp = new Node(Token.LP);
511 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
512 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
513 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
514 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
515 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
516 |       // TODO(user): Mark line number of paren correctly.
517 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
518 |       if (fnName != null) {
519 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
520 |       } else {
521 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
522 |       }
523 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
524 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
525 | 
526 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
527 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
528 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
529 |       }
530 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
531 |       node.addChildToBack(transform(functionNode.getBody()));
532 |      return node;
533 |     }

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 312-320
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
312 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
313 |       Node ret = transform(n);
314 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
315 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
316 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
317 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
318 |       }
319 |       return ret;
320 |     }

===== M0420 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1494-1528
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1494 |     public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1495 |         if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1496 |             try {
1497 |                 int[] indices1 = (int[])getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1498 |                 int[] indices2 = (int[])node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1499 |                 if (indices1 == null) {
1500 |                     if (indices2 != null)
1501 |                         return false;
1502 |                 } else if (indices2 == null) {
1503 |                     return false;
1504 |                 } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1505 |                     return false;
1506 |                 } else {
1507 |                     for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1508 |                         if (indices1[i] != indices2[i])
1509 |                             return false;
1510 |                     }
1511 |                 }
1512 |             } catch (Exception e) {
1513 |                 return false;
1514 |             }
1515 |         } else if (type == Token.INC ||
1516 |                    type == Token.DEC) {
1517 |             int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1518 |             int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1519 |             if (post1 != post2)
1520 |                 return false;
1521 |         } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1522 |             int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1523 |             int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1524 |             if (quoted1 != quoted2)
1525 |                 return false;
1526 |         }
1527 |         return true;
1528 |     }

     * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
     */
Method code:
665 |     public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
666 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
667 |             "The new child node has siblings.");
668 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
669 |             "The new child node already has a parent.");

680 |             prev.next = newChild;
681 |         }
682 |         if (child == last)
683 |             last = newChild;
684 |         child.next = null;
685 |         child.parent = null;
686 |     }

===== M0429 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 817-835
Documentation:

Method code:
817 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
818 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
819 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
820 |       return s;
821 |     }
822 | 
823 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
824 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
825 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
826 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
827 |       // See comments for the same code in strEscape(). Please keep in sync.
828 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
829 |         sb.append(c);
830 |       } else {
831 |         StringUtil.appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
832 |       }
833 |     }
834 |     return sb.toString();
835 |   }

/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {

===== M0434 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 338-392
Documentation:

Method code:
338 |   protected int getChar() {
339 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
340 |       cursor++;
341 |       --ungetCursor;
342 |       if (charno == -1) {
343 |         charno = getOffset();
344 |       }
345 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     for(;;) {
349 |       int c;
350 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
351 |         hitEOF = true;
352 |         if (charno == -1) {
353 |           charno = getOffset();
354 |         }
355 |         return EOF_CHAR;
356 |       }
357 |       cursor++;
358 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
359 | 
360 | 
361 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
362 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
363 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
364 |           continue;
365 |         }
366 |         lineEndChar = -1;
367 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
368 |         lineno++;
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       if (c <= 127) {
372 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
373 |           lineEndChar = c;
374 |           c = '\n';
375 |         }
376 |       } else {
377 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
378 |           continue;
379 |         }
380 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
381 |           lineEndChar = c;
382 |           c = '\n';
383 |         }
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       if (charno == -1) {
387 |         charno = getOffset();
388 |       }
389 | 
390 |       return c;
391 |     }
392 |   }

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.trimEnd(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1324-1339
Documentation:
/**
   * Trim characters from only the end of a string.
   * This method will remove all whitespace characters
   * (defined by Character.isWhitespace(char), in addition to the characters
   * provided, from the end of the provided string.
   *
   * @param s String to be trimmed
   * @return String with whitespace and characters in extraChars removed
   *                   from the end.
   */
Method code:
1324 |   private static String trimEnd(String s) {
1325 |     int trimCount = 0;
1326 |     while (trimCount < s.length()) {
1327 |       char ch = s.charAt(s.length() - trimCount - 1);
1328 |       if (Character.isWhitespace(ch)) {
1329 |         trimCount++;
1330 |       } else {
1331 |         break;
1332 |       }
1333 |     }
1334 | 
1335 |     if (trimCount == 0) {
1336 |       return s;
1337 |     }
1338 |     return s.substring(0, s.length() - trimCount);
1339 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1528-1538
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1528 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1529 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1530 |     int len = s.length();
1531 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1532 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1533 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1534 |         return false;
1535 |       }
1536 |     }
1537 |     return true;
1538 |   }

===== M0469 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.Name.addRef(Ref)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 795-828
Documentation:

Method code:
795 |     void addRef(Ref ref) {
796 |       switch (ref.type) {
797 |         case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
798 |           if (declaration == null) {
799 |             declaration = ref;
800 |             docInfo = getDocInfoForDeclaration(ref);
801 |           } else {
802 |             addRefInternal(ref);
803 |           }
804 |           globalSets++;
805 |           break;
806 |         case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
807 |           addRefInternal(ref);
808 |           localSets++;
809 |           break;
810 |         case PROTOTYPE_GET:
811 |         case DIRECT_GET:
812 |           addRefInternal(ref);
813 |           totalGets++;
814 |           break;
815 |         case ALIASING_GET:
816 |           addRefInternal(ref);
817 |           aliasingGets++;
818 |           totalGets++;
819 |           break;
820 |         case CALL_GET:
821 |           addRefInternal(ref);
822 |           callGets++;
823 |           totalGets++;
824 |           break;
825 |         default:
826 |           throw new IllegalStateException();
827 |       }
828 |     }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.parseJSDocInfo(String,int,int,Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 256-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Parse a JsDoc string into a JSDocInfo.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments (when it returns the new
   * JSDocInfo for the node) and for handling file-level JSDoc comments
   * (@fileoverview and @license).  In this second case, it returns a
   * JSDocInfo if it found another @fileoverview, or null if not.  Also in
   * the second case, all @license text found gets shoved into the
   * fileLevelJsDocBuilder object.
   *
   * @param comment The JsDoc comment to parse.
   * @param lineno The line number of the node this comment is attached to.
   * @param fileLevelJsDocBuilder The builder for file-level JSDocInfo. If not
   *     null, this method parses to a fileOverview JSDocInfo as opposed to a
   *     node-level one.
   * @param fileOverviewInfo The current @fileoverview JSDocInfo, so that the
   *     parser may warn if another @fileoverview is found. May be null.
   * @return A JSDocInfo. May be null if the method parses to the wrong level.
   */
Method code:
256 |   private JSDocInfo parseJSDocInfo(String comment, int lineno, int position,
257 |       Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder,
258 |       JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo) {
259 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
260 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;
261 |     JsDocInfoParser jsdocParser =
262 |       new JsDocInfoParser(
263 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),
264 |                                lineno,
265 |                                position2charno(position) + numOpeningChars),
266 |           sourceName,
267 |           config,
268 |           errorReporter);
269 |     jsdocParser.setFileLevelJsDocBuilder(fileLevelJsDocBuilder);
270 |     jsdocParser.setFileOverviewJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
271 |     jsdocParser.parse();
272 |     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
273 |       return jsdocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
274 |     } else {
275 |       return jsdocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
276 |     }
277 |   }

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 352-456
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
352 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
353 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects) {
354 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
355 |     // that we know to be safe
356 |     switch (n.getType()) {
357 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
358 |       case Token.AND:
359 |       case Token.BLOCK:
360 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
361 |       case Token.HOOK:
362 |       case Token.IF:
363 |       case Token.IN:
364 |       case Token.LP:
365 |       case Token.NUMBER:
366 |       case Token.OR:
367 |       case Token.THIS:
368 |       case Token.TRUE:
369 |       case Token.FALSE:
370 |       case Token.NULL:
371 |       case Token.STRING:
372 |       case Token.SWITCH:
373 |       case Token.TRY:
374 |       case Token.EMPTY:
375 |         break;
376 | 
377 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
378 |       case Token.THROW:
379 |         return true;
380 | 
381 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
382 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
383 |       case Token.REGEXP:
384 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
385 |           return true;
386 |         }
387 |         break;
388 | 
389 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
390 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
391 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null)
392 |           return true;
393 | 
394 |         break;
395 | 
396 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
397 |         // Anonymous functions don't have side-effects, but named ones
398 |         // change the namespace. Therefore, we check if the function has
399 |         // a name. Either way, we don't need to check the children, since
400 |         // they aren't executed at declaration time.
401 |         //
402 |         return !isFunctionAnonymous(n);
403 | 
404 |       case Token.NEW:
405 |         {
406 |           if (checkForNewObjects) {
407 |             return true;
408 |           }
409 | 
410 |           // calls to constructors that have no side effects have the
411 |           // no side effect property set.
412 |           if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
413 |             break;
414 |           }
415 | 
416 |           // certain constructors are certified side effect free
417 |           Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
418 |           if (Token.NAME == constructor.getType()) {
419 |             String className = constructor.getString();
420 |             if (CONSTRUCTORS_WITHOUT_SIDE_EFFECTS.contains(className)) {
421 |               // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
422 |               // side-effects
423 |               break;
424 |             }
425 |           } else {
426 |             // the constructor could also be an expression like
427 |             // new (useArray ? Object : Array)();
428 |           }
429 |         }
430 |         return true;
431 | 
432 |       case Token.CALL:
433 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
434 |         // side effect property set.
435 |         if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
436 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
437 |           // side-effects
438 |           break;
439 |         }
440 |         return true;
441 | 
442 |       default:
443 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()))
444 |           break;
445 | 
446 |         return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
450 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects)) {
451 |         return true;
452 |       }
453 |     }
454 | 
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

   * Covert EXPR_VOID to EXPR_RESULT to simplify the rest of the code.
   */
Method code:
89 |   private void normalizeNodeTypes(Node n) {
90 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EXPR_VOID) {

93 |     }

96 |     // produced by the two parsers.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 972-1046
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
972 |   Node parseInputs() {
973 |     boolean devMode = options_.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
974 | 
975 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
976 |     // individual file parse trees.
977 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
978 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
979 |     }
980 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
981 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
982 |     }
983 | 
984 |     // Parse main js sources.
985 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
986 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
987 | 
988 |     if (options_.tracer.isOn()) {
989 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
990 |           options_.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
991 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
992 |     }
993 | 
994 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
995 | 
996 |     try {
997 |       // Parse externs sources.
998 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
999 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1000 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs_) {
1001 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1002 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1003 |           return null;
1004 |         }
1005 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1006 |       }
1007 | 
1008 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs_) {
1009 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1010 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1011 |           return null;
1012 |         }
1013 | 
1014 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1015 |         if (n == null) {
1016 |           continue;
1017 |         }
1018 | 
1019 |         if (devMode) {
1020 |           sanityCheck.create(this).process(null, n);
1021 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1022 |             return null;
1023 |           }
1024 |         }
1025 | 
1026 |         if (options_.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1027 |             options_.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1028 | 
1029 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1030 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1031 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1032 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1033 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1034 |         }
1035 | 
1036 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1037 |       }
1038 | 
1039 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1040 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1041 | 
1042 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1043 |     } finally {
1044 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1045 |     }
1046 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 162-193
Documentation:
/**
   * For each qualified name N in the global scope, we check if:
   * (a) No ancestor of N is ever aliased or assigned an unknown value type.
   *     (If N = "a.b.c", "a" and "a.b" are never aliased).
   * (b) N has exactly one write, and it lives in the global scope.
   * (c) N is aliased in a local scope.
   *
   * If (a) is true, then GlobalNamespace must know all the writes to N.
   * If (a) and (b) are true, then N cannot change during the execution of
   *    a local scope.
   * If (a) and (b) and (c) are true, then the alias can be inlined if the
   *    alias obeys the usual rules for how we decide whether a variable is
   *    inlineable.
   * @see InlineVariables
   */
Method code:
162 |   private void inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace namespace) {
163 |     // Invariant: All the names in the worklist meet condition (a).
164 |     Deque<Name> workList = new ArrayDeque<Name>(namespace.getNameForest());
165 |     while (!workList.isEmpty()) {
166 |       Name name = workList.pop();
167 | 
168 |       if (name.globalSets == 1 && name.localSets == 0 &&
169 |           name.aliasingGets > 0) {
170 |         // {@code name} meets condition (b). Find all of its local aliases
171 |         // and try to inline them.
172 |         List<Ref> refs = Lists.newArrayList(name.refs);
173 |         for (Ref ref : refs) {
174 |           if (ref.type == Type.ALIASING_GET && ref.scope.isLocal()) {
175 |             // {@code name} meets condition (c). Try to inline it.
176 |             if (inlineAliasIfPossible(ref, namespace)) {
177 |               name.removeRef(ref);
178 |             }
179 |           }
180 |         }
181 |       }
182 | 
183 |       // Check if {@code name} has any aliases left after the
184 |       // local-alias-inlining above.
185 |       if ((name.type == Name.Type.OBJECTLIT ||
186 |            name.type == Name.Type.FUNCTION) &&
187 |           name.aliasingGets == 0 && name.props != null) {
188 |         // All of {@code name}'s children meet condition (a), so they can be
189 |         // added to the worklist.
190 |         workList.addAll(name.props);
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleGet(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 536-583
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     */
Method code:
536 |     void handleGet(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name) {
537 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
538 | 
539 |       Ref.Type type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
540 |       if (parent != null) {
541 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
542 |           case Token.IF:
543 |           case Token.TYPEOF:
544 |           case Token.VOID:
545 |           case Token.NOT:
546 |           case Token.BITNOT:
547 |           case Token.POS:
548 |           case Token.NEG:
549 |             break;
550 |           case Token.CALL:
551 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
552 |                    ? Ref.Type.CALL_GET
553 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
554 |             break;
555 |           case Token.NEW:
556 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
557 |                    ? Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET
558 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
559 |             break;
560 |           case Token.OR:
561 |           case Token.AND:
562 |             // This node is x or y in (x||y) or (x&&y). We only know that an
563 |             // alias is not getting created for this name if the result is used
564 |             // in a boolean context or assigned to the same name
565 |             // (e.g. var a = a || {}).
566 |             type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
567 |             break;
568 |           case Token.HOOK:
569 |             if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
570 |               // This node is y or z in (x?y:z). We only know that an alias is
571 |               // not getting created for this name if the result is assigned to
572 |               // the same name (e.g. var a = a ? a : {}).
573 |               type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
574 |             }
575 |             break;
576 |           default:
577 |             type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
578 |             break;
579 |         }
580 |       }
581 | 
582 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, name, type);
583 |     }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 669-707
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
669 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
670 |         String name) {
671 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
672 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
673 |       // assignments.
674 | 
675 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
676 |       String prefix;
677 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
678 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
679 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
680 |       } else {
681 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
682 |         if (i == -1) {
683 |           return false;
684 |         }
685 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
686 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
687 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
688 |         while (i >= 0) {
689 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
690 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
691 |         }
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
695 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
696 |         // key, so we're done.
697 |         return true;
698 |       }
699 | 
700 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
701 |         parent = n;
702 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
703 |       }
704 | 
705 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
706 |       return true;
707 |     }

/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
960 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
961 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
962 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
963 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node, for instance,
964 |     // is either part of an expression (as a anonymous function) or as

972 |       default:
973 |         return false;
974 |     }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyExpression(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 590-637
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
590 |   private void addNonEmptyExpression(
591 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
592 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
593 | 
594 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
595 |       // TODO(johnlenz) : Enable this when the JsMinifier is corrected.
596 |       // throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
597 |     }
598 | 
599 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children.
600 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
601 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n);
602 |       if (count == 0) {
603 |         cc.endStatement(true);
604 |         return;
605 |       }
606 | 
607 |       if (count == 1) {
608 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
609 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
610 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
611 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
612 |         if (firstAndOnlyChild.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
613 |             firstAndOnlyChild.getType() == Token.DO) {
614 |           cc.beginBlock();
615 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
616 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
617 |           cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
618 |           return;
619 |         } else {
620 |           // Continue with the only child.
621 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
622 |         }
623 |       }
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
627 |       cc.endStatement(true);
628 |     } else {
629 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
630 | 
631 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
632 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
633 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
634 |         cc.endStatement();
635 |       }
636 |     }
637 |   }

     * Detach a child from its parent and siblings.
     */
Method code:
651 |     public void removeChild(Node child) {
652 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
653 |         if (prev == null)
654 |             first = first.next;
655 |         else
656 |             prev.next = child.next;
657 |         if (child == last) last = prev;
658 |         child.next = null;
659 |         child.parent = null;
660 |     }

===== M0559 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 881-994
Documentation:

Method code:
881 |     private void toString(
882 |         StringBuilder sb,
883 |         boolean printSource,
884 |         boolean printAnnotations,
885 |         boolean printType)
886 |     {
887 |         if (Token.printTrees) {
888 |             sb.append(Token.name(type));
889 |             if (this instanceof StringNode) {
890 |                 sb.append(' ');
891 |                 sb.append(getString());
892 |             } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
893 |                 sb.append(' ');
894 |                 sb.append(first.getString());
895 |             } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
896 |                 ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode)this;
897 |                 if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
898 |                     FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode)this;
899 |                     sb.append(' ');
900 |                     sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
901 |                 }
902 |                 if (printSource) {
903 |                   sb.append(" [source name: ");
904 |                   sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
905 |                   sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
906 |                   sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd()
907 |                             - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
908 |                   sb.append("] [base line: ");
909 |                   sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
910 |                   sb.append("] [end line: ");
911 |                   sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
912 |                   sb.append(']');
913 |                 }
914 |             } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
915 |                 sb.append(' ');
916 |                 sb.append(getDouble());
917 |             }
918 |             if (printSource) {
919 |               int lineno = getLineno();
920 |               if (lineno != -1) {
921 |                   sb.append(' ');
922 |                   sb.append(lineno);
923 |               }
924 |             }
925 | 
926 |             if (printAnnotations) {
927 |               int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
928 |               for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
929 |                   int type = keys[i];
930 |                   PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
931 |                   sb.append(" [");
932 |                   sb.append(propToString(type));
933 |                   sb.append(": ");
934 |                   String value;
935 |                   switch (type) {
936 |                     case TARGETBLOCK_PROP : // can't add this as it recurses
937 |                       value = "target block property";
938 |                       break;
939 |                     case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP :     // can't add this as it is dull
940 |                       value = "last local block";
941 |                       break;
942 |                     case ISNUMBER_PROP:
943 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
944 |                         case BOTH:
945 |                           value = "both";
946 |                           break;
947 |                         case RIGHT:
948 |                           value = "right";
949 |                           break;
950 |                         case LEFT:
951 |                           value = "left";
952 |                           break;
953 |                         default:
954 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
955 |                       }
956 |                       break;
957 |                     case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
958 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
959 |                         case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
960 |                           value = "eval";
961 |                           break;
962 |                         case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
963 |                           value = "with";
964 |                           break;
965 |                         default:
966 |                           // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
967 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
968 |                       }
969 |                       break;
970 |                     default :
971 |                       Object obj = x.objectValue;
972 |                       if (obj != null) {
973 |                           value = obj.toString();
974 |                       } else {
975 |                           value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
976 |                       }
977 |                       break;
978 |                   }
979 |                   sb.append(value);
980 |                   sb.append(']');
981 |               }
982 |             }
983 | 
984 |             if (printType) {
985 |               if (jsType != null) {
986 |                   String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
987 |                   if (jsTypeString != null) {
988 |                       sb.append(" : ");
989 |                       sb.append(jsTypeString);
990 |                   }
991 |               }
992 |             }
993 |         }
994 |     }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 49-105
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
50 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
51 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
52 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
53 |     // so we'll allow it.
54 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EMPTY ||
55 |         n.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
56 |       return;
57 |     }
58 | 
59 |     if (parent == null)
60 |       return;
61 | 
62 |     int pt = parent.getType();
63 |     if (pt == Token.COMMA) {
64 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
65 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
66 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
67 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
68 |             continue;
69 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
70 |               ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
71 |             return;
72 |           else
73 |             break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 |     } else if (pt != Token.EXPR_RESULT && pt != Token.BLOCK) {
77 |       if (pt == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 &&
78 |           (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
79 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
80 |         // Fall through and look for warnings for the 1st and 3rd child
81 |         // of a for.
82 |       } else {
83 |         return;  // it might be ok to not have a side-effect
84 |       }
85 |     }
86 |     if (NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()) ||
87 |         !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
88 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
89 |         // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
90 |         // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
91 |         return;
92 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(n)) {
93 |         // we already reported the problem when we visited the child.
94 |         return;
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
98 |       if (n.getType() == Token.STRING) {
99 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
100 |       }
101 | 
102 |       t.getCompiler().report(
103 |           JSError.make(t, n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
104 |     }
105 |   }
